User-requested variant of main e86f6c4 (run 39225913979, 8258.34): the
only change is one fewer speculative token, speculation.steps 3 -> 2 and
draft_tokens 4 -> 3 (topk 1 requires draft_tokens == steps + 1 in SGLang
fc275674). Image 317ab7c7, the SGLang and DeepGEMM overlays, concurrency
192, Decode cap 128 and everything else are main's.

Context: main is Prefill-bound with Decode headroom (ITL 14.3 ms against
the 20 ms SLA, 29.5 of 32 Decode slots per rank in use). With three verify
tokens per request a Decode step processes 96 instead of 128 tokens per
rank (still on the SwapAB MegaMoE path) and runs one fewer draft step,
while the mean accepted length drops. The last time this was measured on
Firmus (run 39035989837, 2026-10-17, Decode-bound configuration) the step
was 8% shorter but the accept length fell 2.21 -> 2.00 and the score fell
6521.98 -> 6320.39; this run checks it under the current Prefill-bound
balance.

The Firmus reference tests no longer hard-code steps 3 / draft 4: the
launch test compares the flags with the config, and the archived
server-info fixture takes the config's step counts like the other current
settings.
